# Specification and governance gates

Companion to [master checklist](../IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md). These cover the original root instructions, module scope/dependencies/implementation tasks/acceptance assertions and shared preambles before source-evidence anchors. They supplement, not replace, the [source contracts](SOURCE_CONTRACTS.md).

All boxes are acceptance gates, not read receipts. For each checked item add an evidence link or explicit disposition. Reference-only rows (including original pending-ledger examples and links) need traceability/disposition, not invented functionality. Detailed status remains in the working parity ledger.
